## Document 013: 115年9月海關進出口貿易初步統計 (2026-10-08)

Extraction status: ok

115 年 9 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
9 月出口 872.2 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.9%，較上年
同月增 60.9%(按新臺幣計算增 68.2%)；第3 季出口 2,449.2 億美元，
較上年同季增 44.6%；累計 1 至 9 月 6,615.5 億美元，較上年同期增
46.2%(按新臺幣計算增 49.1%)。 
（二）進口 
9 月進口 635.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 5.8%，較上
年同月增 51.7%(按新臺幣計算增 58.6%)；第 3 季進口 1,818.0 億美
元，較上年同季增 44.3%；累計 1 至 9 月 5,010.0 億美元，較上年同
期增 41.3%(按新臺幣計算增 43.8%)。 
（三）出入超 
9 月出超 236.3 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 113.4 億美元；
累計 1 至 9 月出超 1,605.5 億美元，較上年同期增 625.4 億美元。

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
由於創新科技應用商機持續擴展，推升相關供應鏈出貨動能，加上
消費性電子新品備貨需求與產品價格上漲效應之挹注， 9 月出口較
上年同月增 330.0 億美元(+60.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 391.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增
198.6 億美元(+1.0 倍)， 其中電腦及其附屬單元增157.4 億美元(+1.1
倍)、儲存媒體增 17.6 億美元(+2.2 倍)、交換器及路由器增 10.5
億美元(+1.1 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 311.3 億美元(歷年單月次高)， 較上年同月增97.1
億美元(+45.3%)，其中積體電路增 93.9 億美元(+46.1%)、印刷電
路增 1.4 億美元(+33.7%)、 電容器及電阻器增0.8 億美元(+32.0%)。 
3. 礦產品出口 22.6 億美元，較上年同月增 10.5 億美元(+86.4%)，其
中石油煉製品增 10.1 億美元(+99.0%)。 
4. 機械出口 27.4 億美元(歷年單月次高)，較上年同月增 6.4 億美元
(+30.7%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.7 億美元(+61.0%)、未列名
具特殊功能機械增 0.6 億美元(+73.6%)。 
5. 基本金屬及其製品出口 26.9 億美元，較上年同月增 5.0 億美元
(+22.9%)，其中銅及其製品增 2.5 億美元(+65.1%)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.4 億美元(+11.7%)。

（二）與上年同期比較 
1. 第 3 季出口較上年同季增 755.9 億美元(+44.6%)，以資通與視聽產
品增 367.6 億美元(+55.0%)、電子零組件增 308.4 億美元(+51.2%)
增加較多。 
2. 累計 1 至 9 月出口較上年同期增 2,089.8 億美元(+46.2%)，其中資
通與視聽產品增 1,222.2 億美元(+72.4%)、電子零組件增 726.6 億
美元(+45.7%)。

等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈全球分工與出口引申需求擴增，電子零組件、資通產品
及半導體設備進口維持強勁，加上國際農工原料行情處於高檔，9
月進口較上年同月增 216.7 億美元(+51.7%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口440.5 億美元， 較上年同月增163.9億美元(+59.3%)，
其中電子零組件增 112.6 億美元(+82.5%)、電腦之零附件增 19.4
億美元(+1.5 倍)、礦產品增 17.1 億美元(+38.9%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 128.0 億美元， 較上年同月增 36.0 億美元(+39.1%)，
其中資通設備增 18.4 億美元(+75.0%)、半導體設備增 12.4 億美元
(+34.9%)，交通運輸設備減 1.4 億美元(-46.4%)。 
3. 消費品進口 62.1 億美元，較上年同月增 16.5 億美元(+36.0%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.4 億美元(+5.3 倍)、筆電增 0.5 億
美元(+43.8%)，藥粧用品減 1.2 億美元(-16.6%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月對東協出口創新高，對美國、陸港為單月次高 
1. 對美國出口 274.6 億美元，較上年同月增 141.5 億美元(+1.1 倍)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 124.1 億美元(+1.3 倍)、 電子零組件增10.1
億美元(+1.3 倍)、機械增 2.2 億美元(+42.3%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 206.2 億美元，較上年同月增 47.8 億美元
(+30.2%)，其中電子零組件增 34.9 億美元(+30.5%)、資通與視聽
產品增 5.7 億美元(+39.0%)、機械增 1.9 億美元(+43.7%)。 
3. 對東協出口 188.8 億美元，較上年同月增 91.2 億美元(+93.4%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 49.7 億美元(+2.2 倍)、電子零組件增 32.0
億美元(+63.3%)、礦產品增 4.8 億美元(+1.4 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 45.3 億美元，較上年同月增 7.7 億美元(+20.6%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 5.7 億美元(+34.1%)、電子零組件增 2.2 億美
元(+56.6%)，基本金屬及其製品減 0.8 億美元(-20.5%)。 
5. 對日本出口 30.7 億美元，較上年同月增 3.6 億美元(+13.4%)，其
中電子零組件增 1.5 億美元(+11.0%)、基本金屬及其製品增 0.6 億
美元(+32.5%)、機械增 0.5 億美元(+29.1%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月對美國出口 2,077.8 億美元，較上年同期增 773.1 億美
元(+59.3%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,600.9 億美元，增 355.3 億美
元(+28.5%)， 對東協出口1,339.6 億美元 ， 增463.6 億美元(+52.9%)，
對歐洲出口 421.8 億美元 ， 增138.4 億美元(+48.8%)， 對日本出口277.7
億美元，增 58.3 億美元(+26.6%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
9 月自中國大陸與香港進口 135.0 億美元 ， 較上年同月增50.1 億美元
(+59.1%)，自南韓進口 116.3 億美元，增52.6 億美元(+82.5%)，自東
協進口 106.0 億美元，增 52.6 億美元(+98.4%)，自日本進口 64.3 億
美元，增 17.7 億美元(+38.0%)，自歐洲進口 50.2 億美元，增 3.5 億
美元(+7.4%)，自美國進口 48.1 億美元，增 6.5 億美元(+15.5%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 9 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較 ， 自東協增77.6%、
自南韓增 65.8%、自中國大陸與香港增 42.8%、自日本增 25.7%、自
美國增 21.4%、自歐洲增 10.8%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）主要國家/地區今年 1-8 月外貿景氣普遍走升，以南韓(1-9 月)出口年
增 56.8%最為強勁，香港、新加坡亦分別年增 49.0%、39.8%；中國
大陸、美國出口分別年增 19.3%及 14.6%，德國(1-7 月)、日本各增
9.7%、8.3%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步上揚，累計 1-8 月以香港進口年增 50.5%較
突出，新加坡、中國大陸分別年增 37.3%、27.0%，南韓(1-9 月)、德
國(1-7 月)分別增 19.7%、10.0%，日本、美國各增 7.8%、4.2%。

### Page 7

7
圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）受惠於人工智慧(AI)、高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續擴展，
加以步入傳統外銷旺季 、產品價格上漲效應，9 月出口 872.2 億美元，
年增 60.9%，連續 35 個月正成長；進口635.9 億美元，年增51.7%，
主因 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口引申需求擴張，及國際原物料行情居
高；出、進口值及出超 236.3 億美元齊創單月新高。累計 1 至 9 月出
口年增 46.2%、進口增 41.3%。
（二）9 月主要貨品出口普遍較上年同月成長，以資通與視聽產品增 1.0 倍
最佳，包括顯示卡、伺服器、儲存媒體、 交換器及路由器等出口均
呈倍數增加 ；其次為礦產品增 86.4%，主因全球油品供應偏緊支撐價
格維持高檔；電子零組件隨 AI 應用及消費性電子新品備貨需求升溫，
增 45.3%，機械、電機產品亦各增 3 成上下。累計 1 至 9 月資通與視
聽產品、電子零組件出口併計增 59.5%，占總出口比重 79.0%。

（三）主要市場方面，9 月對美國、東協出口值分創歷年單月次高、新高，
年增率各為 1.1 倍及 93.4%，合計對總出口增加貢獻達 7 成；對中國
大陸與香港、歐洲、日本出口各年增 30.2%、20.6%、13.4%。累計 1
至 9 月對五大市場出口值皆為歷年同期最高，其中對美國、東協均
提前刷新全年紀錄 ， 分別年增59.3%、52.9%，對歐洲出口年增48.8%，
對陸港、日本各增 28.5%、26.6%。
（四）展望未來，全球貿易政策及地緣政治情勢變化等，仍增添總體 經濟
不確定性，惟隨人工智慧應用 持續深化，國際雲端服務業者 及各國
加速 AI 基礎建設投資，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供應鏈需求擴增，
加以科技新品備貨、中國大陸及歐美年底採購旺季將屆， 均有利挹
注我國外銷動能，第 4 季出口可望延續成長態勢。

公司簡介 
聯發科為台系 IC 設計龍頭廠商，為全球第
五大 IC 設計業者 (僅次於 Qualcomm、
Broadcom、nVidia)，著墨智慧型手機、智
慧裝置與電源管理晶片的發展。

主要客戶 ：全球手機品牌以及智慧家電品牌 
主要競爭對手 ：Qualcomm、展訊、華為海
思、Broadcom、Marvell、聯詠、瑞昱

王文雯 wenwen.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

聯發科 (2454 TT) 
ASIC 業務引領營運成長

永豐觀點 
ASIC 業務營收規模優於預期，2028 年還有第二個 ASIC 客戶步入量產(屬於 AI 機
櫃設計等級，公司能承接的工作範圍更多)，帶動營運顯著成長。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：ASIC 業務貢獻優於預期，研究部上修獲利，預估聯發科 2026 年稅後
EPS 72.8 元(原預估 70.5 元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 142.78 元(原預估 132.52 元)，
2028 年稅後 EPS 321.22 元(原預估 312.93 元)。聯發科 ASIC 業務進展優於預期，
可望帶動後續營運強勁成長，研究部對聯發科維持買進，目標價由5570 元調升至
5800 元(由 2027~2028 年 EPS 均值與 25 倍評價)。

ESG 評析 
聯發科企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。

### Page 2

2

聯發科為 台系 IC 設計龍頭廠商 ：聯發科為全球第五大 IC 設計業者 (僅次於
Qualcomm、Broadcom、nVidia)，著墨智慧型手機、智慧裝置與電源管理晶片的
發展 。 在智慧型手機晶片方面的主要競爭對手為Qualcomm、 展訊及華為海思等 ，
在智慧家庭、AIoT 等應用領域則以 Broadcom、Marvell、聯詠及瑞昱等廠商為
主。客戶主要透過代理商供應產品，終端客戶以全球手機品牌(OPPO、VIVO、小
米、Samsung、榮耀等)以及智慧家電品牌為主。 
3Q26 財報優於預期 ：聯發科 3Q26 營收 1680.58 億元(+10%QoQ，+18%YoY)，
毛利率 46.3%， 營業利益率16.1%， 稅後淨利278.82 億元(+15%QoQ，+11%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 17.37 元，財報優於預期，主要是營收超標、營業費用控管得宜所致。 
聯發科 3Q26 財報各個細項表現如下：(1) 營收季增 10%，超逾目標值 1522 億元
~1598 億元(QoQ+0%~+5%)，三大產品線表現均優於預期，得益於專案挹注程
度與供應鏈產能爭取進度較佳，三大產品線以Smart Edge Platform 挹注最顯著。
聯發科 3Q26 三 大 產 品 線 營 運 動 能 如 下 ：Smart edge platform 季增
20%(+68%YoY， 營收佔比58%)、 電源晶片(+1%QoQ，+18%YoY， 營收佔比5%)、
手機營收滑落 1%QoQ(-17%YoY，營收佔比 37%)，其中 Smart edge platform
季增幅度超逾公司原預期的，QoQ+5%~+9%，公司前次法說即已表示將有多項
聯網與車載項目於 3Q26 進入量產 ， 現在看來5G modem/AI tablet(天璣 9500 導
入三星平板電腦 Tab S120/AI PC / 車載晶片貢獻更優，並且 TV SoC 內含 DRAM
漲價亦有貢獻。 電源晶片亦優於公司原預期的持平， 主要是來自運算與資料中心市
占率提升。手機僅季減1%，落於公司原預期的 QoQ-5%~+0%的上緣，應受惠於
旗艦晶片天璣 9600 出台所致。(2) 毛利率 46.3%，與前季相仿，落於目標值區間
44.5%~47.5%區間。(3) 營業費用 507 億元(+7%QoQ)，營業費用成長性<營收
成長性，反映營業費用控管得宜。(4) 聯發科 3Q26 存貨 1535 億元(+56%QoQ)，
庫存天數由 2Q26 的 102 天增至 127 天，主要是因應 ASIC 業務拓展。

### Page 3

3

4Q26 營收展望優於預期：聯發科預估 4Q26 營收 2101 億元~2218 億元(以美元
對新台幣匯率 31.7 元計算)，QoQ+25%~+32%，YoY+40% ~+48%，毛利率目
標值區間下滑 1%至 43.5%~46.5%，營業費用率 24%~28%，反映營業利益率
16%~23%，優於過往三年多落於 14%~19%水準。聯發科 4Q26 展望細項如下：
(1) 4Q26 營運由 ASIC 業務驅動，(2) 4Q26 毛利率目標值區間下滑 1%，主要是受
ASIC 業務毛利率<公司均值，以及晶片價格因製造成本上升而出現轉嫁事宜的綜
合因素影響。(3) ASIC 業務的營業利益率>公司均值，故帶動公司 4Q26 營業利益
率回升。(4) 公司預期 2026 年營收成長性，超逾原預期的 YoY+5%~+9%。研究
部預估聯發科 4Q26 營收 2180 億元(＋30%QoQ，+45%YoY)，毛利率 45%，稅
後獲利 405.2 億元(+45%QoQ，+77%YoY)，稅後 EPS 25.25 元。 
ASIC 業務貢獻值優於預期：聯發科本次法說揭示 ASIC 業務貢獻值優於預期：(1) 
公司預期 2026 年 ASIC 業務 25 億美元(優於前次法說僅預期 >20 億美元)，
Zebrafish 已在 09/2026 開始小量出貨。(2) 2027 年 ASIC 業務 160 億美元(市佔
率 20%)，落於公司原預期的市占率 15%~20%與貢獻 120 億~160 億美元的上
緣。(3) 第二款 ASIC Humufish 維持預計將於 1Q28 步入量產，目前該平台部分
die 業已 tape out 完成，主要 die 也在 tape out 進行當中，先進封裝仍將優先採
用 Intel EMIB 。(4) 2027 年的營業利益率將>20%，反映營運槓桿效益提升。(5) 
第三代 ASIC(若順利應於 2029~2030 貢獻)則目前尚未定義最終規格。 
再者，公司亦表示第二個 ASIC 業務(搭載 NVLink Fusion) 預計 2028 年開始出貨
(前季法說僅表示尚與潛在客戶洽談中，本季已明確表示將於 2028 年出貨)，此為
新客戶(公司表示非中國客戶，故應表示該客戶非先前傳言的 Bytedance)。協助單
顆晶片設計與進入 NVLink Fusion 機櫃級設計的差異，在於幫客戶將晶片納入整
合進 Nvidia 的 AI 機櫃系統，需要增加高速互聯/相容性/記憶體架構/封裝協同設
計，以與 Nvidia 的交換器或網路系統偕同運作，反映聯發科能承接的工作範圍更
多，亦即接到單一客戶的商業價值應該更多。但因為此為聯發科首次導入 AI 機櫃
等級的業務，相關客戶與細項內容，均尚難知曉，故研究部本次財務預估尚未將此
估算在內。 
維持買進：考量 ASIC 業務貢獻優於預期，研究部上修獲利，預估聯發科 2026 年
營收 6874 億元(+15%YoY)，毛利率 45.87%，營業利率 16.9%，稅後獲利 1168
億元(+11%YoY)，稅後 EPS 72.8 元(原預估 70.5 元)，2027 年營收 1.17 兆元
(+71%YoY)， 毛利率43.77%， 營業利率21.5%， 稅後獲利2292 億元(+96%YoY)，
圖一：聯發科庫存天數與營收走勢

### Page 1

Idea
Chroma Ate Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Momentum Seen Sustained into
2027
SL T, photonics (transceiver+CPO), and power testing should
continue to drive growth into 2027, with contributions from FT
handlers and burn-in ovens likely emerging as well. Stay OW.
3Q26 miss on GM: See details in Results Summary
Growth momentum seen sustained into 4Q26 and 2027: Management indicated
that the strong business momentum observed in 3Q26 is likely to stay intact going

forward, driven by both power testing and semi & photonics businesses. Normalized
GM remains at ~60% (2026 guidance of ~60% GM implies GM back to ~60% in
4Q26 vs. 57.5% in 3Q26).
• Power testing: 60-70% of revenue is AI-related for 2026, and it is expected
to be sustained into 2027. HVDC testing tools could be the major driver, and
it has already received orders from customers.
• SLT: 9M26 revenue doubled on a YoY basis. SL T demand used to focus on

one major customer, but management indicated that it has added AVGO and
MRVL as new customers. Moreover, demand in 4Q26/1H27 should be mostly
related to inference applications, including CPU/ASIC/LPU.
• Photonics: 9M26 revenue grew by 4x on  a YoY basis. Demand from
transceivers should remain solid and CPO should grow notably in 2027.
Chroma focuses on Insertion 3/4o and shipments for the mass production
line next year  are seen reaching 4-5x vs. that for the pilot line this year.

Moreover, it has added another customer for a pilot run in 9M27 and mass
production in 4Q27. At the industry level, if insertion is excluded from mass
production, Insertion 3 tool demand should be higher. Opportunities will
likely be much larger when CPO penetrates the scale-up network.
• New businesses: FT handlers have been qualified by multiple HPC/CSP
customers and orders have been placed. Burn-in ovens have secured the first
CSP customer, with more HPC customers expected to follow next year.

These should be new addressable markets for Chroma in the coming years.
Stay OW: We believe Chroma should continue to benefit from the AI compute/
networking/power testing opportunities due to its strong customer relationship,
solid execution and outstanding technology expertise. Our PT of NT$2,800 is based
on 40x 2027e P/E, implying a PEG of 0.6 (vs. 1.0 over the past decade).

Results Summary
3Q26 miss on GM:
Off revenue of  NT$17,926mn (+32% QoQ/+180% YoY), GM contracted 3ppt QoQ, to
57.5%, below our estimate at 60.5% and consensus at 62.7%, mainly owing to a less
favorable product mix.
Opex of NT$3,141mn (opex ratio 17.5%) was lower than our estimate at NT$3,797mn (opex
ratio 21.2%) and consensus at NT$3,321mn (opex ratio 21.7%), reflecting operating
leverage.
This led to operating profit of NT$7,167mn (OPM up 1.2ppt QoQ, to 40.0%), 2% higher

than our estimate at NT$7,040mn (OPM 39.3%) and 14% higher than consensus at NT
$6,281mn (OPM 41.0%).
With a narrower non-op gain of NT$186mn, net profit reached NT$5,647mn (EPS NT
$13.37), 2% short of our estimate at NT$5,790mn (EPS NT$13.71) and 6% short of
consensus at NT$5,317mn (EPS NT$12.59).

A Closer Look
Test instrument and ATS segment looks set to reach another
record high in 2026
Revenue for the segment increased 55% YoY to NT$10,546mn (37% of the mix) in 2025.
We estimate it will rise 118% YoY to NT$24,564mn (40% of the mix) in 2026. The ever-
increasing power consumption of AI clusters should continue to drive demand for higher
spec testing tools. Along with our forecast of a 75% CAGR for global power supply unit

demand in the coming three years (see our report), this should remain a major driver for
Chroma, in our view.

Multiple drivers in the semiconductor business
System-level testing  (SL T) business
SL T should benefit from the capex cycle of NVIDIA's Rubin generation GPU, AMD's MI450
GPU and more ASIC accelerators.  Management pointed out that discrete testing tools are
still preferred  by major customers.  We are also seeing more HPC chip designers adopting

the system level testing process to make sure defects won't be incorporated into the AI
systems. We believe Chroma, as the first mover in this market, should benefit from
increasing chip volume, as well as higher TDP.
Metrology business
The metrology business should continue to benefit from current advanced packaging
architectures from its foundry and OSAT customers. It is confident that the performance
of its tools can win future architecture projects, which our industry checks suggest should

have higher metrology density.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Exhibit 4: Chroma –  Offerings for Metrology in Advanced Packaging
Source: Chroma
Chroma's metrology equipment employs white light interferometry technology. The basic
working principle is that white light is emitted on the reference mirror and the sample,
which are placed at the same distance from the light source. The reflected beams from
the reference mirror and sample surface are then received by a light sensor.
Interference occurs when two light beams collide, which could cause  them to either

strengthen or weaken, depending on the phase of the two light beams. Thus, by
measuring the intensity of the combined light beams, the distance between light sensor
and multiple points on the target surface can be calculated through an algorithm. This can
then be used to measure the dimensions of the structure or the alignment of layers.
Exhibit 5: Concept of Light Interference
Source: Keyence
Exhibit 6: Setup of Interferometer
Source: Wikipedia
CPO contribution to start from 2026

Chroma has been qualified for optical engine testing in Insertion 3 and system-level
testing in Insertion 4, and there might be incremental opportunities if supply chain players'
progress falls behind for testing equipment in Insertion 2. Over the longer term, Chroma
also  aspires to participate in the PIC optical/electrical/reliability testing in Insertion 1.

FT handlers to add another revenue stream
In HPC testing, Chroma used to focus mostly on the SL T side, although it did unveil its first
HPC FT handler earlier this year and is now going through the qualification process. We
believe this is a natural extension of its expertise and customer relationships accumulated
in SL Ts. We expect Chroma to gain more traction in FT handlers for AI HPCs in 2027.

Morgan Stanley Research 7
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chroma Ate Inc. (2360.TW)
Base case, 40x 2027e P/E. We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we think it better
captures Chroma's earnings growth momentum. The stock traded at an average valuation of
~19x P/E during 2014-25 with an operating profit CAGR of 20%, implying a PEG of 1.0. We think
our target multiple of 40x is justified by the 68% operating profit CAGR we project for

2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected macro conditions
n Stronger semi and photonics and power testing demand
n Margin improvement via better product mix
n Better opex control
Risks to Downside
n Macro slowdown
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower progress in the advanced packaging capacity expansion
n Weaker consumer electronics and automobile related demand
n Margin contraction arising from lack of revenue scale and increasing opex

the next 12-18 months.

12-18 months.

## Document 178: 261102_ms_GUC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261102_ms_GUC.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 15
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
Axion CPU demand remains strong and on track: We noted  in a prior report  that
demand for Google's Axion CPUs, which GUC helps design, remains robust.
However, we now see further upside in both 2027 and 2028 from our previous
forecast. Also, some investors have questioned whether the Axion3 schedule has
been pushed out. Our  recent supply chain checks suggest that Axion3 has already
reached wafer-in and is on track for wafer-out in mid-2027. Axion CPU could reach 4-

5mn units of  Axion2 equivalent CPU in 2027, given that Axion3 includes two
computing dies but also adopts TSMC 3nm process. We now forecast volume at
4mn units of Axion2 and 500k units of Axion3 in 2027, then 3mn units of Axion3 in
2028. We project ASP at US$700 and US$1400, respectively.
We also see upside surprise from Cobalt CPU: We believe the CPU demand upside
is coming not just from Axion but also Microsoft's Cobalt CPU. GUC continues to
get more front-end wafer support from TSMC; given strong CPU demand, we now

forecast >1mn units of Cobalt 200 in 2027 with ASP of ~US$700. Our industry
checks also suggest that Cobalt 300 is on track for mass production in 2028, which
might adopt TSMC's 2nm process. By assuming it could also be a dual-computing-die
CPU, we currently forecast the ASP to be ~US$1800 with 500k units in 2028.
3Q26 revenue beat from CPU customers' demand pull-forward and new
customer: The company posted its  3Q26 results: revenue beat our estimate

strongly (by 23%);  the upside comes mostly from the largest CPU customer's pulling
demand forward  (50-55% of 3Q26 total revenue) and also the new US AI customer,
which has adopted the 5nm process and SoW (system-on-wafer) at TSMC. In 4Q26,
we expect a major Chinese auto company to start to contribute revenue. With the
ongoing robust CPU demand, we now expect 4Q26 revenue to be up 20% QoQ. See
Exhibit 3  for our 3Q26 earnings review.
Reiterate OW and raise price target to NT$8600: This 37% increase mostly

reflects the upside we have seen from CPU demand. However, aside from CPU
demand, we also see strong revenue from Tesla, Meta, Horizon, etc.   in 2028. Thus,
we expect 2025-28 revenue CAGR to reach 120%. Our new price target implies P/E
of 52x for 2027e and 27x for 2028e, which we think is justified by its strong revenue
growth.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Strong chip demand from CPU  and AI business
Axion CPU should be the largest customer for GUC in 2026-2028: We therefore now
forecast only 1.5mn units of Google Axion2 CPU in 2026, given the demand pull-in. By
assuming chip ASP at ~US$700, we now see 2026 Google CPU contributing slightly more
than US$1000mn.
For 2027, we have been seeing strong rises in demand expectations for Axion CPU,
including  the ramp of the next-generation Axion3. We now expect Axion CPU revenue of

close to US$3.5bn, including 4mn units of Axion2 and 500k units of Axion3. In addition,
we also expect the chip ASP of Axion3 to double from Axion2 given the increase of 3nm
computing dies from one to two.
For 2028, we now forecast  3mn units of Axion3, slightly lower than Axion2's annual run
rate of 4mn units, mostly also driven by the doubling of computing dies.
CPU turnkey revenue carries a low gross margin for Axion CPU – likely around 10% –
because this is a “Turnkey 2 or 3” project, where GUC provides advanced packaging design

support (e.g., silicon interposer and ABF substrates) but does not own the photomasks for
the ASIC designs. We also expect margin for Axion3 to be slightly lower than for Axion2,
given higher chip ASP or larger project size.
Cobalt demand could  also grow 3-4x in 2027: GUC also helps with Microsoft's Cobalt
200/300 CPU. We now forecast at least US$1bn of GUC revenue contribution from
Cobalt 200 and Maia 200 in 2027. Also, our industry checks also suggest that Cobalt 300

is on track for mass production in 2028, which might adopt TSMC's 2nm process. By
assuming it could also be a dual-computing-die CPU, we currently forecast the ASP to be
~US$1800 with 500k units in 2028.
Meta ASIC (MTIA 600): In our AI supply chain tracker, we noted our belief that GUC
could win one of the Meta ASIC projects, led by the Rivos team, and we now think it might
be MTIA 600, based on supply chain checks. The AI ASIC targets tape-out in 1H27, with

potential CoWoS out in 2028.
Note: these forecasts are from Asia Pacific research and incorporate supply chain and
industry checks.

Estimate revision summary
We raise   our EPS estimates 17% for 2026, 18% for 2027, and 50% for 2028: Our
increases come mostly from the strength in CPU as well as other AI projects' contribution,
including Meta ASIC, US design house adopting SoW, etc. With turnkey revenue share
seen increasing in both 2027 and 2028, we now assume that gross margin will fall from
21.4% in 2026 to 15.4% in 2028. However, we expect firmer operating margin this year,

thanks to a policy change regarding employee bonus; although this assumption dips in
2027, it rebounds in 2028 because of larger revenue scale with better operating leverage.
The same trend is visible in  net margin.

Price target rises 37%, to NT$8,600
This reflects our 2027-28 estimate revisions. Although we are cutting our terminal growth
rate assumption from 5% to 4.5% because of margin dilution, our other  key valuation
assumptions are unchanged, including  cost of equity of 9.2% (a beta of 1.2, a risk premium
of 6%, and a risk-free rate of 2%), and intermediate growth rate  of 14.5%.
Our bull and bear case values rise by similar magnitudes, to   NT$11,680  and NT$5,300,

respectively.

Relative valuation
Our price target of NT$8,600 implies 52x our 2027 EPS estimate, around +1 SD from
GUC’s historical NTM P/E since 2013. We maintain our view that the design service market

will proliferate with AI ASIC outgrowing AI GPU in the long term, and therefore believe
that further re-rating would be justified with project wins likely to come. Despite slower
recovery of non-AI business, we continue to appreciate GUC’s growing exposure to cloud
AI semis (including AI accelerators, AI server CPU, and BMC chips).

NT$11,680.00
71x 2027e EPS
Multiple leading-edge NRE projects
successfully translate into volume
production with healthy margin proﬁles.

Many AI chipsets or other projects start to
proliferate with substantial end demand,
with customers' interest in migrating to
more advanced nodes. Networking switch
demand is stronger than expected, while
SSD controller project contributions are
sustainable in 2026. 3/5nm turnkey revenue
growth is stronger than expected in 2026
and beyond.
NT$8,600.00
52x 2027e EPS
Networking switch turnkey projects volume
remains stable. A few new leading-edge
turnkey projects start to show decent

shipments. TSMC's leading-edge tape-out
numbers increase steadily, while some
"turnkey 2/3" (TK2 and TK3) projects
contribute to NRE and turnkey revenue. CSP
(AI) revenue contribution should be the
major growth driver for 2026 and 2027, as
well as the auto chip and SSD controller
projects.
NT$5,300.00
32x 2027e EPS
AI chipsets and other projects lack concrete
end demand, resulting in delayed tape-outs.
TSMC's leading-edge tape-out numbers
decrease or some customers turn directly to

TSMC. BMC and SSD project losses arise
from customers' in-sourcing. Fiercer-than-
expected talent competition leads to higher
opex to retain R&D engineers. High volume
turnkey projects carry lower-than-expected
gross margin.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

陳祐竹 
(886-2)2563-1666 
yorick.chen@firstsec.com.tw

衛司特(6894) 
買進, TP 500 (潛在報酬 19.05%)  
買進 
Price(10/22):420 
Target Price：500

衛司特 26H1 獲利翻倍，EPS 達 9.7 元，逼近去年全年水準，主因
AI 伺服器帶動 PCB 高階電鍍製程由可溶性銅球轉向不溶性陽極鈦
網的剛性替代，帶動陽極鈦網營收年增約 50%，加上東南亞新廠
陸續投產使 BOO 業務穩健放量，以及半導體設備銷售與維護大幅
成長。展望後市，隨著 PCB 產業往高階多層板推進、層數與複雜
度提升，而面板廠 「鋁改銅」 趨勢確立，產業規格升級使含銅廢液
處理需求大增， PCB、面板及半導體業客戶積極擴產，加上切入
京東方供應鏈，跨廠複製動能使未來成長具想像空間，BOO 再生
金屬服務 、 陽極鈦網耗材及半導體電解設備與維護三大業務皆具高
成長性，轉投資城鋒科技也將成為新的成長動能。綜合以上，研究
部預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 21/31.28 元，以未來四季 EPS 計算，
給予本益比 20x，目標價 500 元，投資建議為買進。

⚫ 26H1 獲利翻倍 三大業務齊揚推升營運 
衛司特 26Q2 營收 5.91 億元，QoQ+14.7%、YoY+71.5%，
稅後淨利 1.24 億元，QoQ+15.4%、YoY+124.6%，EPS 5.19
元；26H1 營收 11.06 億元，YoY+65.8%，稅後淨利 2.31 億
元，YoY+98.5%，EPS 9.7 元。受惠 AI 伺服器發展引發的高
階電鍍製程材料替代 ，26H1 陽極鈦網營收 YoY 約 50%，PCB
客戶東南亞廠區陸續投產 、 加上高層數高密度蝕刻帶動含銅廢
液量增加，使 26H1 BOO 業務 YoY+30~40%，而 26H1 半導
體設備銷售與維護達 1.07 億元，已接近去年全年 1.12 億元。
高毛利的半導體設備比重提升，加上平均銅價 YoY 約 40%，
帶動 26Q2 毛利率年增 2.95 百分點至 37.07%。

⚫ 規格升級疊加擴廠潮及新廠滲透 多動能推升 2027 年成長 
衛司特 26Q3 營收 6.12 億元，QoQ+3.7%、YoY+74.5%。
RecoCell BOO 業務面向 PCB 及光電面板產業 ， 截至9 月底 ，
共有 67 個 PCB 案場及 11 個光電面板案場 ，PCB 產業方面，
隨著產業往高階多層板推進 ， 層數與複雜度提升使每單位面積
蝕刻出的含銅廢液濃度顯著增加 ， 疊加客戶積極擴增國內產能
與東南亞新廠陸續投產，帶動大量含銅廢水處理需求；此外，
傳統可溶性磷銅球會隨電鍍耗損縮小且易產生汙泥，在 AI 伺

除息日 2026/03/25 服 器 高 深 寬 比 通 孔 電 鍍 要 求 趨 嚴 、 高 速 基 板 材 料 升 級 至
M8/M9 等級驅動下，尺寸安定的不溶性陽極鈦網替代傳統可
溶性磷銅球，已成為不可逆的產業趨勢。光電面板產業方面，
隨著顯示規格升級，帶動面板廠在高品質與高世代產線推動
「鋁改銅」 ，使含銅廢液處理需求大增，今年公司正式打入中
國面板龍頭京東方供應鏈，京東方新產線全面導入銅製程，衛
司特於今年 6 月開始進駐並貢獻月營收 700~800 萬，目前公
司僅服務京東方一個廠區，預計今年底或明年切入第二個廠，
並規劃在未來 2~3 年爭取 7~8 個廠區合作 ， 後市具想像空間 。
BOO 再生金屬服務約 30%與銅價連動，全球 AI 算力與電力
基礎建設的發展使銅價長線具剛性支撐 ， 高檔震盪的銅價環境
有利於衛司特業務發展。綜合以上，研究部預估 2027 年
RecoCell BOO 業務成長 20%， 陽極鈦網可望維持高達約50%
之成長動能。

半導體產業方面，衛司特銷售 RecoCell 電解回收設備與維
護，2025 年半導體營收 1.12 億元，隨台灣半導體大廠積極擴
產 ， 預估2026 年半導體營收成長至 1.8 億元 ，2027 年進一步
躍升至 3 億元，2026~2027 年連續兩年皆維持 60%~70% 的
高年增率。

⚫ 轉投資布局深化循環經濟生態系 
轉投資方面，衛司特今年初以 1.93 億元取得城鋒科技 51%股
權，主要經營廢鉛蓄電池處理與回收，擁有 1000 噸的鉛酸電
池月處理許可，26H1 實際月處理量約 400~600 噸廢電池，
26H1 挹注衛司特營收 9300 萬元；為了提升效益，26H2 城鋒
暫停運轉以執行產能改善計畫，預計於年底將月產能提升至
1000 噸並實現轉虧為盈 ， 後續將進一步導入新設備 ， 待27Q1
設備建置完成，將申請擴充月處理量至 4000~5000 噸，盼
27H2 獲批後開始放量。此外，衛司特董事會於 9 月決議斥資
6 億元 ， 以每股60 元認購天瑛公司現金增資案 ， 取得20.83%
股權，天瑛公司為台灣最大規模的硫酸銅製造商，衛司特將結
合自身的銅廢水回收技術與天瑛的特化品精煉技術 ， 共同打造
高純度硫酸銅的封閉式全循環供應鏈。

綜合以上，研究部預估衛司特 2026 年營收 23.41 億元，
YoY+69.9%， 毛利率37.1%， 稅後淨利5 億元 ，YoY+91.1%，

EPS 21 元；2027 年營收 31.64 億元，YoY+35.1%，毛利率

⚫ 結論與建議 
衛司特 26H1 獲利翻倍，EPS 達 9.7 元，逼近去年全年水準，
主因 AI 伺服器帶動 PCB 高階電鍍製程由可溶性銅球轉向不溶
性陽極鈦網的剛性替代，帶動陽極鈦網營收年增約 50%，加
上東南亞新廠陸續投產使 BOO 業務穩健放量，以及半導體設
備銷售與維護大幅成長。展望後市，隨著 PCB 產業往高階多
層板推進 、 層數與複雜度提升 ， 而面板廠 「鋁改銅」 趨勢確立，
產業規格升級使含銅廢液處理需求大增 ，PCB、 面板及半導體
業客戶積極擴產，加上切入京東方供應鏈，跨廠複製動能使未
來成長具想像空間，BOO 再生金屬服務、陽極鈦網耗材及半
導體電解設備與維護三大業務皆具高成長性 ， 轉投資城鋒科技
也將成為新的成長動能。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026/2027
年 EPS 21/31.28 元 ， 以未來四季EPS 計算 ， 給予本益比20x，
目標價 500 元，投資建議為買進。

주가 추이

3Q26 매 출액 과 영 업 이익 은 비현 금 성 비용 항목 개 선 으로 시장 기대치
를 상 회 했 으나, 차량 판매 감소와 인 플 레 이션 등으로 연간 가이 던 스 는 
하 향 되 었 습니 다. 올해 생산 차질 에 따라 이 연된 신차 판 매를 ’27년에 
확대하며 매출/이익 회복을 가시 화 하 는 부분 에 주목 필 요 합니다. 
매출 성장은 금융부문이, 영업이익은 자동차부문이 기여 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 45.1조원(-3.5% 이하 Y oY), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%)을 기록하며 시장 기대치를 상회했다. 파업으로 인한 생산 차질
로 3Q 판매량 92.1만대(-11.3%)에 그치며 자동차부문 매출은 큰 폭으
로 감소(33조원, -10.0%)했으나, 금융부문 매출이 취급 자산 증가로 큰 
폭으로 성장(+27.8%)했다. 반면, 영업이익은 금리 상승에 따른 금융부문 
영업비용 확대 영향과 기타부문 실적 부진 발생과 달리 자동차부문은 전
년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소와 판매보증 충당부채 전입
액 감소 등 우호적 환율효과로 큰 폭의 증가세(+24.8%) 기록했다. 
’26년 가이던스 하향, 구조적 반등 요인은 아직 
3Q 실적 발표에서 주목할 부분은 가이던스 하향과 현금흐름이다. 연간 
판매목표를 연초 415.8만대에서 400만대로 하향했고 영업이익률 목표
는 6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 0.8%p 하향했으며, ’26년 투자계획은 
17.8조원에서 16조원으로 낮췄다. 특히, 투자계획 하향은 차량 판매 매
출 감소 대비 인센티브, 원자재 가격 상승 등으로 현금흐름이 악화된 
부분에 기인한 것으로 판단된다. 실적발표에서 전사적 원가 최적화 활
동 추진을 강조했으나, 이는 좀 더 장기적 측면에서 효과가 가시화될 
것으로 판단되며, 인플레이션 및 원화절상 상황 고려 시 구조적 반등은 
단기적으로 쉽지 않을 수 있다. 
절치부심의 2027년, 목표주가 600.000원 유지 
그러나 매크로 상황을 제 외 하 면  ’26년 하반기 생산 차질에 따른 신차 
판매효과가 ’27년 으 로 이연된 것 이 라 는 점에 주목 필요하다.   ‘북미 HEV, 유럽 
BEV’ 전략 하에서 엘란트라, 투싼 풀세인지, GV80, G80 등 고수익 HEV 차종 
중심 미국 수요 대응을 통해 Mix 개선하는 모습이 확인되고, 생산이 본 격 화 된 
아이오닉3 유럽 판매 확대 시 중국 로 컬 업 체 들 의 공세를 방 어 하 는 모 습 을 
확인하는 것이 가 능 할 것으로 판 단 한 다. 노 후 차 종 중심 운 영 에 따른 판매 둔화 
이외에 아직 근 원 적 판매 경쟁력 저하로는 판단하지 않는다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 30일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

## Document 257: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43.pdf

### Page 1

(012450) 
한화에어로스페이스 
2026.10.30

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 1,660,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 760,000원(10/29)

 3Q26 연결 매출액은 6조 943억원(-6.0%yoy), 영업이익은 4,520억원(-
46.2%yoy, OPM 7.4%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센 1조 1,614억원을 하회.  
 지상방산의 손익이 크게 악화. 영업이익은 217억원(OPM 1.7%) 기록. 주요 
납품이 4분기에 집중된 가운데 일회성 비용 약 500억원(대전사업장 복구·
정상화 약 300억원, 국내 사업 충당금 약 160억원)과 환율 영향이 겹침.   
 다만 내용을 살펴보면 수출 사업의 수익성 훼손은 크지 않았다는 판단임.  
 일회성 제외 영업이익은 약 717억원(OPM 5.8%)이며, 일회성 비용은 대전
사업장이 국내 물량을 전담한다는 점에서 전액 국내 귀속으로 추정. 내수 
OPM을 일회성 제외 -5% 내외로 가정하면, 내수 영업이익은 약 -826억원
(일회성 포함 OPM -12.7%), 수출은 1,043억원(OPM 17.6%)으로 추정됨. 
 1Q26 대비로는 국내 마진 하락이, 2Q26 대비로는 수출 물량 감소가 가장 
크게 작용(도표 참고). 국내는 저마진 사업 매출이 늘고 고마진 반복 양산 물
량이 줄며 일시적으로 믹스가 악화. 수출은 폴란드 등 주요 인도가 정체되어 
매출이 감소한 가운데 저수익 사업 중심으로 믹스가 악화된 영향. 
 4Q26부터 회복 구간 진입 전망. 폴란드 K9 2차 실행계약 물량의 현지 인
도가 본격화되며, 회사는 2026년 폴란드 K9 30문 이상, 천무 발사대 60대 
이상(*연초 40대에서 상향) 인도를 계획. 수출입 데이터상으로도 폴란드·루
마니아향 출하가 대규모로 진행 중이며 폴란드향 부속류 수출도 정상적으로 
이어지는 것으로 확인. 일회성 소멸과 고마진 수출 집중으로 수출 OPM은 
30% 내외로 회복할 것. 다만 국내는 정상 가동(2027년 상반기 목표) 전까
지 한 자릿수 초반 마진에 머물 것으로 전망. 
 중장기 수익성 우려도 크지 않다는 판단. 2027~2029년 매출 연 15% 성
장 가이던스는 유지됐고, 내년 폴란드 K9·천무 인도 물량은 모두 올해 대비 
증가할 계획. 폴란드 사업 이후 높아진 수출 마진 레벨은 후속 사업에서도 
유지될 전망. 수주는 중동 지정학 불안으로 지연되고 있으나 대공체계 등 문
의가 이어지고 있어, 2027년부터 중동이 수주의 중심이 될 것으로 기대. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 166만원을 유지함. 3Q26 지상방산 
쇼크는 국내 적자와 수출 물량 공백이 겹친 일시적 결과로, 수출 경쟁력 훼
손으로 보기 어려움. 실적 충격에 따른 주가 조정은 분할 매수 기회로 판단.  
 한화에어로스페이스의 주가는 4월 7일 사상 최고가 153.7만원에서 10월 
29일 76.0만원으로 -51% 조정 받은 상황. 12MF P/E 기준 14.3x, 27E 
P/E는 13.9x로 밸류에이션 관점에서도 매력적인 구간 진입. 그동안 3분기 
실적 우려 등으로 부진했다면, 이제는 실적 바텀아웃과 대형 수주 랠리 속 
다시 한 번 반등을 기록할 차례. 동사를 방산업종 Top-pick으로 유지함.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금두산에너빌리티(034020) 3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡는다면 지금3Q26 Review_ 바닥을 잡_62bfa2bd.pdf

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

Company Brief 
유틸리티/건설 
Analyst 문경원, CFA 
kyeongwon.moon@meritz.co.kr 
RA 이동석 
dongseok.lee@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 Review: 바닥을 잡는다면 지금  
믹스 개선에 따른 이익률 개선 조금씩 관찰 중 
3Q26 연결 영업이익은 2,612억원(+90.6% YoY) 을 기록하며 컨센서스(2,455억원)
를 소폭 상회했다. 밥캣(1,674억원)이 컨센서스(1,761억원)를 소폭 하회한 가운데, 
에너빌리티 부문 영업이익(+179% YoY, OPM 6.6%) 이 호실적을 이끌었다. 환율 하
락에도 불구, 가스터빈, 원전 등 고수익성 프로젝트의 비중 상승이 주요 원인이다. 
3Q26 누적 수주는 10.0조원(+85.5% YoY) 을 기록했다. 4Q26 가스복합 EPC 수주 
등을 감안하면 연간 가이던스(13.3조원) 달성은 무난하다. 한수원과의 협상 결렬로 
이목이 집중된 체코 두코바니 시공 수주는 2027년 인식될 확률이 높아 보인다. 주간
사가 바뀌더라도 이미 프로젝트에 참여 중인 동사를 배제하기는 쉽지 않다.

대미투자 이후, 연말~연초 수주 모멘텀에 집중 
연말 연초에 다음과 수주 모멘텀이 집중되면서, 주가 상승을 이끌 전망이다. 
1) 대형원전: 정부 자금 중심의 Westinghouse LLI(장납기 기자재) 발주가 몰린다. 
우선 미국 DOE(에너지부)가 제공한 대출을 기반으로 미국 수주가 기대된다.  폴란드 
정부 역시 최근 LLI 발주 작업을 시작했다. 한편, 한국 정부는 연말까지 100억 달러 
규모의 대미 투자 송금을 협의 중인데, 이 역시 DOC(상무부) 주도의 대형 원전 건설
을 위한 LLI 발주에 쓰일 가능성이 높다 
2) SMR: 오래 기다려왔던 Nuscale 향 수주가 시작되고 있다. 우선 29일 모듈 1기 
제작 계약을 체결했다. 현금이 부족한 Nuscale이 용기를 냈다는 건, 곧 Nuscale-
TVA 간 PPA 가 임박했음을 의미한다. 본격적인 수주는 PPA 직후 예상된다.  
3) 가스 터빈: 2026년 초 해외 수주(7기)는 2029년까지 인도 계획이다. 이후 2030
년 슬랏 예약을 완료하고 2031년 물량을 논의 중으로 보인다. 지금은 2030년 슬랏
에 대한 PO가 나와 수주잔고에 인식할 타이밍이다. 역시 수출 물량으로 추측한다.

성장에 대한 기대가 사라진 밸류에이션, 지금이 바닥 
투자의견 Buy를 유지, 할인율 상승을 반영하여 적정주가를 109,000원으로 -17% 
하향 조정한다. 금리 상승, 환율 하락, 트럼프 지지율 하락 등 Top-down 리스크에 
최근 조정을 받았지만, 추가적인 주가 하락 폭은 제한적일 전망이다. EV/선행 수주잔
고 지표는 현재 1.0배 수준까지 하락하며, 원전의 성장성이 주목받지 못했던 
2021~2024년 수준에 근접하게 내려왔다.  
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 30

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 109,000원

## Document 292: AI可以免疫高利率？泡沫末期，市场往往充斥着幻想

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI可以免疫高利率？泡沫末期，市场往往充斥着幻想.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

当分析师⾼喊 " ⾼利率不影响 AI 繁荣 " 时，历史的回响令⼈不安 —— 这与 2000 年互联⽹泡
沫、 2008 年⾦融危机前⼣的叙事如出⼀辙。数据已在⽆声⽰警： Alphabet 、 Meta 、微软等
科技巨头表外承诺总规模达 4.11 万亿美元， 90 天内暴增近 1 万亿；纳斯达克 100 本⽉市值蒸
发逾 1.2 万亿美元。表外承诺不随股价消失， AI 商业化收⼊能否赶在债务浪潮到来前完成追
赶？
摘要：
当债券收益率攀升，分析师们却挥挥⼿说 "AI 繁荣不受影响 "—— 这是经典的泡沫逻辑。历史上每
⼀次重⼤繁荣的终结，⽆不伴随着融资条件的收紧；⽽每⼀次泡沫末期，市场都会涌现出各种
理由，声称 " 这⼀次不同 " 。 AI 热潮能否真正免疫⾼利率，答案或许已在数字中悄然浮现。
以 Alphabet 、 Meta 、微软、亚⻢逊为核⼼的超⼤规模云计算企业及 Nvidia 、 Broadcom 、
SpaceX ，其披露的表外承诺总规模已达 4.11 万亿美元 —— 仅在约 90 天内新增承诺接近 1 万亿美

元。与此同时，这些公司账⾯上的实际债务和租赁负债合计约 1 万亿美元，意味着每 1 美元表内
负债背后，对应着超过 4 美元未在资产负债表上显⽰的承诺。与之形成对照的是，纳斯达克 100
指数成份股本⽉市值合计蒸发逾 1.2 万亿美元。
表外承诺不因股价涨跌⽽消失，到期必须兑付。当前的核⼼悬念在于：AI 商业化收⼊能否在下
⼀轮承诺浪潮到来之前实现追赶。⽽随着⾼利率对 AI ⼦⾏业的冲击逐步显现，市场正在从狂热
⾛向分化，资本配置趋于审慎。
泡沫末期的惯常叙事：⾼利率 " 不重要 "
Rockefeller International 主席 Ruchir Sharma 指出，每当债券收益率上⾏，总有分析师声称 AI 繁
荣可以独善其⾝，⽽这恰恰是经典的泡沫逻辑 —— 资⾦价格永远重要，且有充分理由相信，当
下⽐以往任何时候都更为如此。
2000 年互联⽹泡沫期间，市场流⾏的神话是 " 互联⽹产品的巨⼤需求 " 使科技公司摆脱了利率等
普通经济规律的约束。 2008 年危机前⼣，分析师则试图论证 " 利率上升不会刺破泡沫 " ，或最多

只会放缓⽽⾮逆转资产价格涨势，因为企业盈利处于 "30 年来最佳⽔平 " 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

写评论
如今相似的论调以 AI 之名卷⼟重来：⼏个百分点的利率，对于那些正在创造历史性价值增⻓的公
司⽽⾔⽆⾜轻重；美联储以区区基点计量的加息，不可能压制那些志在赢得 AI 军备竞赛的公司的
⽀出意愿。
Sharma 强调，此轮利率环境与 1990 年代的可⽐性有限。当前利率不仅处于⾼位，更是在近 25 年
历史性低利率之后短期内急速攀升 ——这⼀剧烈的机制转换本⾝⾜以造成⾦融压⼒。已有迹象
浮现：投资者要求以更⾼收益率为 AI 供应链中较弱的借款⽅融资。
表外承诺急速膨胀，单季新增逾 5700 亿美元
当前 AI 基础设施投资狂潮的规模，在财务数据中呈现出惊⼈的轮廓。
据最新季报数据，以 Alphabet 、 Meta 、微软、亚⻢逊为核⼼的超⼤规模云计算企业及 Nvidia 、

Broadcom 、 SpaceX ，其披露的表外承诺总规模已达 4.11 万亿美元，较摩根⼠丹利 8 ⽉末统计的
3.12 万亿美元增加近 9920 亿美元 —— 约 90 天内新增承诺接近 1 万亿美元。如果把摩根⼠丹利此前
统计中纳⼊的、基于新闻报道⽽⾮正式财报披露的表外承诺也计算在内，总额将达到 4.29 万亿美
元。
会计处理上的不透明，正在引发直接的市场反应。
分析机构 Needham 指出，⼀旦将表外负债纳⼊计算， Meta 的企业价值将提升 35% 。 Meta 在财报
披露后信⽤违约掉期触及历史⾼位，随即宣布最⾼ 950 亿美元的股权融资计划，其中 375 亿美元
已完成，另有约 600 亿美元的 ATM 项⽬待执⾏。 Amanda Lynam 亦指出，部分企业已明确表⽰需
要在市场趋于饱和之前提前锁定资本 —— 这⼀表述被认为是 Meta 股权融资的直接反应，且未必
是最后⼀例。
摩根⼠丹利曾于 8 ⽉警告，这些承诺在算⼒供应受约束期间具有 " 战略价值 " ，但 " 若供需在预期

之前趋于正常化，企业将不得不为过剩产能买单，或试图重新谈判合同条款 " 。 Alphabet 股价本
⽉经历创纪录的单⽇暴跌，亚⻢逊持续⾛低，纳斯达克 100 指数成份股本⽉市值合计蒸发逾 1.2
万亿美元。
Sharma 的判断简洁⽽直接： AI 相关债务发⾏规模预计将在 2026 年超过 6000 亿美元，较 2024 年
增⻓⼗倍；⽽过去三个世纪每⼀次的重⼤繁荣，都终结于⾦融条件收紧之中。这场可能是史上
最具变⾰性的科技热潮，同样未必能够抗拒资⾦⾼价的引⼒。

## Document 358: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United  States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3  according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever -volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2 -0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues . As anticipated by our  Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2).  
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%).  According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2),  driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export gr owth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports ( -1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q).  Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, gr owth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026 , 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate ( AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheles s 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI -related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

Head of Advanced Economies Team, France  
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe

## Document 362: Eurozone_ Improving business sentiment, driven by tech, is spreading to other sectors (2026-10-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone_ Improving business sentiment, driven by tech, is spreading to other sectors (2026-10-14).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

14 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
EUROZONE: IMPROVING BUSINESS SENTIMENT, DRIVEN BY TECH, IS SPREADING TO OTHER SECTORS
Stéphane Colliac & Tarik Rharrab
*LATEST OBSERVATION: 5 OCTOBER 2026  **ESTIMATE
The September PMI surveys confirm that growth in the Eurozone is getting stronger, boosted in particular by new export orders, which are
still rising. Sectors that were already performing well – technology and capital goods (including for defence) – are continuing to grow, while

those that had been struggling until now – aeronautics, consumer goods and financial services – are seeing their production indices recover.
This strong performance is particularly noteworthy, given that it has not yet been undermined by higher inflation.
The heatmap shows the PMI index readings: a reading above 50 indicates expansion, while a reading below 50 signals contraction compared to the previous
month. For each indicator, sectors are ranked in descending order based on their September 2026 reading.

Shades of red represent values above 50, while shades of blue represent values below 50: the intensity of the colour increases the further away they are
from this threshold.
Production growth spreads to more sectors
The PMI surveys on business sentiment confirm that business activity in the Eurozone is expanding at a faster pace and spreading to more sectors.
Household-consumption-related sectors (goods and services), which were adversely affected in the spring by the outbreak of the conflict in the Middle

East, do not appear to be negatively impacted by the rising inflation observed since August. The aeronautics sector, where production has mainly been
limited by supply constraints, is also showing signs of improvement.
SOURCE: S&P GLOBAL, BNP PARIBAS

These trends, although still moderate, come alongside a drive to upgrade equipment, fuelled by investment in digital technology, electrification
(which is supporting the automotive sector) and defence. The momentum in technology goods and services further strengthened in September.
The production index for computer and electronics hit a five-year high, while the software services production index stayed close to its July peak.

Financial services are also enjoying a significant improvement, reflecting the healthy lending momentum despite interest rates starting to rise
(see our analysis).
Growth driven by exports
Exports drove growth in the first half of 2026, both across the Eurozone as a whole (see our analysis) and among its key members (notably
Germany). This momentum is also increasing, as indicated by the ‘new export orders’ component of the various PMI indices. As was the case with

production, there has been a particularly noteworthy increase for capital goods (technology and machinery) and related services, particularly digital
services. This trend is playing out more broadly, as, in September, new export orders rose across all sub-sectors, with the exception of intermediate
goods and consumer goods, where contraction recently eased. There has been a significant improvement in the transport sector, particularly in
the aeronautics industry, which has major markets in Asia. Recent data on French exports show that aeronautics exports to this region are rising

once again.
The convergence between rising production and increasing export-order backlogs shows that Eurozone growth is based on an increasingly solid
foundation. Inflationary pressures are not holding it back for the time being.

## Document 390: Kicking and screaming_ Child care costs persist Child care remains a constraint for many families’ budgets, with the average share of monthly child care 8% of a household’s pay. 2 

Source PDF: all report pdf/Kicking and screaming_ Child care costs persist Child care remains a constraint for many families’ budgets, with the average share of monthly child care 8% of a household’s pay. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

13030323     1

Economy 
Kicking and screaming: Child care costs persist 
20 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Child care remains a growing financial burden for many households, even as growth in the median payment has moderated, 
according to Bank of America payments data. More families are paying more than $2,000 per month for care, suggesting 
affordability pressures are becoming increasingly concentrated among households facing the highest expenses. 
• Child care cost pressures vary significantly across the country. Nashville and Jacksonville, FL posted the fastest year-over-year 
growth among major metro areas, while New York and Washington DC saw the biggest decrease in median payment growth, 
according to Bank of America payments data. 
• Limited supply may continue to constrain access to child care for some households. Child care employment fell 0.7% year-over-
year in September, suggesting provider capacity remains under pressure and could continue to affect household finances and 
labor force participation.

Child care costs outpace rent and medical services 
Child care remains a significant expense for many families. In September, the growth in child care and other school-related fees 
surpassed other major household expenses such as rent and medical services, according to the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) 
(Exhibit 1). 
Yet, while child care prices continue to rise in official inflation data, median household payments have largely moderated since 
February, according to Bank of America data (Exhibit 2). More importantly, the growth in the number of households paying for 
child care has fallen since the start of 2025. 
Exhibit 1: The cost of child care has surpassed other services such as 
rent in the consumer price index  
Consumer price index by category (monthly, seasonally adjusted, YoY%)

Source: Haver Analytics  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Exhibit 2: The median child care payment per household was up 
1.0% year-over-year (YoY) in September but the number of 
households paying for child care fell 1.0% YoY 
Median child care payment per household and number of households 
(monthly, three-month moving average, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Fewer lower-income households are paying for child care 
Why is Bank of America payment growth trending in the opposite direction of the Consumer Price Index? Households are likely 
changing the amount of care they use, which is reflected in slower growth in the number paying for child care. As more

households shift into lower-cost care (school-age, public pre-K, subsidized or free after-school programs), median payments can 
fall even as prices continue rising.  
The decline in the number of households paying for child care may also reflect families seeking alternatives to formal care 
arrangements. Some may be relying more heavily on relatives, shifting parents’ work schedules or dropping out of the workforce 
altogether to care for their children in response to elevated costs. This could explain why, among all income groups paying for 
care, the number of lower-income households has seen the biggest decline, falling 2.0% year-over-year (YoY) in September 
(Exhibit 3). 
Exhibit 3: The number of lower-income households paying for child care has fallen the most, down 2.0% YoY in September 
Number of households with child care payments (monthly, three-month moving average, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
More families face larger monthly bills 
Yet even as fewer households pay for child care, the share of those shelling out more than $2,000 per month has increased 
substantially (Exhibit 4). In fact, it has more than doubled since 2023, reaching 7.7% in 2026 year-to-date. Meanwhile, the share 
of those paying $1,000 or less has declined, suggesting higher monthly payments are becoming more common even as the 
national median grows more slowly. 
Exhibit 4: The share of households paying >$2000 a month for child care has more than doubled since 2023 
Monthly child care payments by amount (annual average, share of households, %)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Rising costs take a bigger bite out of households’ paychecks 
What makes child care “affordable”? According to the U.S. Department of Health and Human Services, child care is considered 
affordable if it accounts for no more than 7% of family income. Yet, in Bank of America data, the average child care cost was 8% 
in September.  
While an 8% share may not appear dramatically above the federal affordability benchmark of 7%, it represents an average across 
households’ income levels. For families in high-cost metro areas or those paying more than $2,000 per month, the burden is 
likely considerably higher.

Where child care costs are climbing 
Child care pressures also differ substantially by location. In Bank of America payments data, Nashville posted the fastest YoY 
median payment growth in September, followed by Jacksonville, FL and Phoenix (Exhibit 6). This dispersion of growth across the 
country suggests child care cost pressures are no longer confined to historically expensive coastal markets.  
Interestingly, New York and Washington DC saw the sharpest decline in growth this year. Why might this be? The supply side of 
the market may help explain why – for example, New York City recently introduced universal Pre-K.1  
Exhibit 6: Nashville and Jacksonville, FL saw the greatest increase in median child care payment growth among select cities in September 
Median child care payments by core-based statistical area in September (YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data

Fewer providers, higher costs 
As of September 2026, employment in child care services was down 0.7% from a year earlier, suggesting the sector’s workforce 
recovery has stalled. Fewer workers can constrain the number of available places or the hours providers offer. That may leave 
families facing longer searches, fewer workable arrangements or continued price pressure. In turn, access to care can influence 
parents’ ability to enter, remain in or expand their participation in the workforce. 
Exhibit 7: Employment within child care services fell 0.7% YoY in September 
Employees by sector (monthly, seasonally adjusted, YoY%)

Source: Haver Analytics  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
What are the economic implications of rising child care costs? 
Child care costs may have important labor market implications for families with young children. Of those households paying for 
child care in 2026, more than half of them were Older Millennials (52%), followed by Younger Millennials (20%) and Gen X (16%), 
according to Bank of America payments data. This means child care costs weigh more heavily on Older Millennials. 
The share of Older Millennial households receiving more than one paycheck has continued to grow from a year earlier, but the 
pace of that improvement has moderated over the past three months (Exhibit 8). Given that this group accounts for the majority 
of households paying for child care in Bank of America payments data, they may offer early signs of how affordability affects 
their employment decisions. 
Exhibit 8: The share of Older Millennial households paying for child care that have more than one person with a paycheck has declined since 2021  
Share of households with a child care payment with more than one person with a paycheck (%, monthly, three-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Child care challenges can weigh on women’s employment 
According to data from the American Time Use Survey, women continue to perform significantly more child care and household 
work than men, even as their participation in paid work has increased. This suggests that persistent gaps in access and 
affordability may have disproportionate consequences for women’s employment. Indeed, the percentage of women who cited 
child care as a reason for temporary unemployment was nearly three times higher than men’s in 2026, though the average fell 
from 2025, according to data from the BLS. 
Yet labor force participation data indicates a more nuanced picture. Despite persistent child care pressures, mothers are 
participating in the workforce at rates that remain above their 2019 averages.

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Bank of America credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card holders 
making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards is excluded. Data regarding 
merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial services 
companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS), 
which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
If applicable, the consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level.  
If applicable, any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a 
deposit account or credit card. Any reference to aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-
peer, cash and checks.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank of Thailand (BOT) (October 28)

We expect the BOT to maintain its policy 
rate at 1.00% at its 28 October meeting, 
extending its pause for a fourth 
consecutive decision as it balances 
inflationary pressures against uneven 
and subdued economic growth. Our view 
is aligned with fixed market income 
pricing. Headline inflation rose anew to 
2.8% yoy in September 2026, driven by 
higher energy, food, and core price 
increases, but remained within the upper 
end of the central bank’s 1 -3% target 
range. As the BOT is still likely to look 
through what it views as temporary, 
supply-driven inflationary pressu res, it 
will likely refrain from tightening policy, 
and keep interest rates accommodative 
to support the soft and uneven growth 
cycle.

European Central Bank (October 29)

We expect the ECB to extend its pause, 
keeping the deposit facility rate at 2.5%. 
Market participants will instead focus on 
the central bank’s assessment of rising 
French and Italian bond yields and 
widening spreads versus Bunds. While 
we do not expect dir ect references to 
individual countries, the ECB is likely to 
signal that recent developments in 
France reflect the country’s weaker 
fiscal and debt profile rather than a 
broader systemic concern. Policymakers 
will seek to differentiate between a 
localised bond sell -off and a Eurozone -
wide market disruption, justifying their 
decision to monitor developments rather 
than implement specific measures at this 
juncture. Meanwhile, inflation continues 
to rise, with little relief expected in 
upcoming readings given elevated 
global fuel prices. 
Bank of Japan (October 30)

The Bank of Japan is expected to keep 
the overnight call rate unchanged at 1.25% 
at this meeting, pausing after the 25bp 
hike in September. The BOJ is likely to 
assess the impact of the previous rate 
hike before taking further action. The 
recent stabilisat ion of the yen also 
reduces the urgency of another hike at 
this meeting.

The tightening bias should remain intact. 
Growth and inflation remain broadly on 
track, leaving room for the BOJ to revise 
up its FY2026 –FY2027 GDP and CPI 
forecasts. Wage growth has also 
remained strong, with Rengo reportedly 
targeting wage increases of 6% in 2027, 
reinforcing the BOJ’s confidence that 
underlying inflation can be sustained 
around its 2% target. We continue to 
expect the policy rate to rise to 1.50% in 
December 2026 or January 2027, 
followed by a further increase to 1.75% 
by 2Q 2027.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Export growth is expected to accelerate 
from 53.0% yoy in August to 55.0% in 
September, in line with the pickup in 
China’s export growth from 25.0% to 
36.3%. As a key re-export hub for China,

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 23, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Hong Kong should benefit from robust 
AI-related electronics trade, with 
electrical machinery, equipment and 
parts exports to Hong Kong accounting 
for 20.6% of China’s total exports. 
Import growth is also expected to remain 
firm, supported by resilient expo rt 
orders and gradually improving 
investment sentiment, sustaining 
demand for intermediate and capital 
goods.

Singapore

Singapore’s industrial production (IP) 
likely remained in expansion for a 13th 
consecutive month in September 2026, 
although we expect growth to moderate 
to 7.6% yoy from August’s strong 15.4% 
yoy pace. IP growth continued to be 
underpinned by the electronics and 
precision engineering clusters, which 
has been key drivers of Singapore’s 
manufacturing strength, reflecting 
sustained demand for artificial 
intelligence-related hardware. However, 
performance across clusters remained 
uneven, with chemicals and biomedical 
manufacturing likely contracting, and 
tempering overall gains.

South Korea

The preliminary estimate for 3Q26 GDP 
is likely to show growth remaining solid 
at 3.2% yoy , slightly below 3.7% in 2Q 
and 3.8% in 1Q. Nominal GDP growth is 
likely to remain in double digits, 
significantly outpacing real GDP growth, 
thanks to improved terms of trade amid 
rising memory export prices. By 
expenditure, exports picked up in 3Q in 
both nominal and real terms, supported 
by stronger semiconductor shipments. 
Investment also appeared robust, as 
reflected in the equipment investment 
estimation index and machinery order 
index. Private consumption, however, 
appeared to weaken amid the sharp 
KOSPI correction since July following  
the introduction of leveraged ETF 
regulations. Our full -year GDP growth 
forecasts of 3.5% for 2026 and 2.4% for 
2027 remain on track.

Taiwan

The preliminary estimate for 3Q GDP is 
likely to show growth remaining strong 
at 12 –13% yoy , close to 12.9% in 2Q, 
albeit below the peak of 15.4% in 1Q. 
Exports maintained strong double -digit 
growth in 3Q (44.6% yoy  in nominal 
terms and 21.1% in real terms), supported 
by robust shipments of semiconductors, 
servers and other AI -related products. 
Machinery investment also sustained 
strong double-digit growth, underpinned 
by higher capacity utilisation in the ICT 
sector and a resulting increase in capital 
goods imports. Private consumption 
appeared to have strengthened further, 
supported by stock market gains, 
improving wage growth, and the 
associated wealth and income effects. 
Construction investment remained the 
weak s pot, weighed down by elevated 
inventories in the new-home market and 
the ongoing need for inventory digestion. 
We maintain our 2026 and 2027 GDP 
growth forecasts at 11.6% and 5.6%, 
respectively, with risks tilted to the 
upside.
